codex

Phase 0 is **conditional** ??it only runs when the receipt-prompt hook injected an informational context block (v1.3.1).

integrity result |

plugins\mccp\commands\plan.md:25:| 5 | PLAN-CODEX GATE | Auto-invoke Codex adversarial review, inject result, write receipt, hand off to `/mccp:prp-implement`
 |

ream receipt (v1.3.1 informational path). When that block is present, auto-recover deterministically before Phase 1 ANALYZE runs. When absent, skip this phase
 entirely.

중간 결과로 두 가지 중요한 정정이 보입니다. `plan`의 확인 대기는 실제 계약이라 자동 체인을 막을 가능성이 높지만, “상태 전달 수단이 없다”는 주장은 `work.md`가 명시한 JSON 아티팩트 handoff와 충돌합니다. 또한 저장소에는 이미 work completion 
지표 모듈이 있어 “로그로만 관찰 가능”이라는 근거는 그대로 인정하기 어렵습니다.

C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:287:Exit 2 has one case that is **not a failure at all**: when stdout carries `reason:"SANTA_SINGLE_PASS_
ACTIVE"`, `MCCP_REVIEW_SINGLE_PASS` is set to one of its three reasons and santa-loop deliberately does not open a round in a single-pass window (review-loop-
bypass M1 / DD5). The refusal happens **before** `beginRound`, so the ledger is untouched and the cap is not consumed ? running the loop later costs nothing. 
No receipt is written either: `mccp-santa-review` is produces-only, and a "did not fire" receipt would need a schema that accepts a receipt with no round tall
y. The audit anchor is the loud refusal plus the absence of a ledger entry. To run the loop, unset `MCCP_REVIEW_SINGLE_PASS`.

C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:911:**Both branches are load-bearing, and they check different things.** `SEAL_EXIT` answers "did the sea
l complete?"; `SEAL_VERDICT` answers "what did it seal?" A seal can succeed (exit 0) while recording `divergent`, and branching on the exit code alone would p
ush under an anchor that says the review did not converge ? the same class of defect one layer up. Step 4's NICE is a read of the final round; the sealed verd
ict is derived from the whole ledger (round count, the recorded round verdict, distinct reviewer ids), so a disagreement means the ledger does not support wha
t Step 4 read ? an empty ledger, a final round that is not NICE, or fewer than two distinct reviewers.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:913:The seal deliberately does **not** re-gate on the reviewers' `verdict` strings. Those stopped being a
 judgment input at Step 4 (a `FAIL` raising nothing blocking leaves the round NICE by design), so a seal that still failed on them would not be "stricter" ? i
t would be answering a different question and contradicting the gate, which is exactly what it did before santa-adjudication M1 fixed it. What the seal keeps 
is the axis it can check independently: two distinct reviewer ids, counted a second time from the ledger.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:917:**The ack opens the push; it does not rewrite the verdict.** `MCCP_SANTA_DEGRADE_ACK` is judged in ex
actly one place ? `seal` ? and this step only reads the `degradeAck` boolean it returned. Re-reading the env here would give two interpretations of one variab
le, and this repo has repeatedly found that shape of defect (it is why `--lane` is re-derived at a single CLI point). The sealed verdict stays `degraded`, the
 receipt keeps `review_verdict='divergent'` alongside `meta.santa_model_degraded=true`, and the ack itself is recorded as `santa_degrade_ack` + `santa_degrade
_ack_reason`. That matters on a machine with no external CLI, where *every* run degrades and the ack ends up living in `settings.json`: if the ack flipped the
 verdict, the count of degraded runs would be permanently zero and the metric would lose the thing it measures. Left as-is, the ratio keeps being counted, and
 that ratio is the measured case for installing `codex` here.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:919:`$SEAL_JSON` carries `reportPath` / `proofPath` / `receiptPath` / `verdict` / `degraded` / `degradeRe
ason` / `degradeAck`; Step 7 reports them.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:962:- The cap binds at the **ledger index**, not just here: `record` and `verdict` refuse an index `begin
-round` never opened, so ignoring a refusal and launching reviewers produces no ledger entry and no verdict *at that index*. Two things it does **not** preven
t, and this file claims neither: the reviewer tokens being spent (launching a reviewer is an LLM act, with nothing for a shell to intercept), and reuse of the
 last already-FINAL index ? `record --round <cap-1>` still succeeds, because restricting `record` to `OPEN` rounds is judgement lifecycle and belongs to P1.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:963:- The loop's **first** ending condition is Step 4.5, not the cap: a round whose surviving blocking is
sues all point at the previous round's patch ends there, and the ledger records that ending with its own reason so the two endings can be told apart afterward
s. The cap is the safety net underneath it, and reaching it is itself recorded as an ending.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:964:- The terminator's kill switch is `MCCP_SANTA_TERMINATOR`, registered in docs/ENVIRONMENT.md §11 alon
gside the other santa toggles. It turns off both wiring points together ? the Step 4.5 judgement and the `begin-round` marker precheck ? and that document own
s its values, defaults and failure mode.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:965:- Exactly one reviewer runs on the **blind evidence lane** each round: repository root plus target pa
ths, no file bundle and no pre-summary, with a fixed instruction not to treat any handed narrative as fact. The assignment is decided by `santa/lanes.js` and 
re-checked by `record --lane`, so the command body cannot pick a lane on its own. What that check establishes is that the lane came from the oracle; it does *
*not** establish that the blind reviewer's prompt truly carried no bundle ? no shell can observe what reached a model. Verification of *that* is by outcome di
stribution (the two lanes' co-missed rate), not by the stamp. Coverage is sealed into the receipt as two present-only integers, `meta.santa_blind_records` and
 `meta.santa_blind_rounds`; `santa_blind_rounds === santa_rounds` is the mechanical reading of "every round had at least one reviewer that received no bundle"
.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:967:- A round whose reviewers all resolve to the **same model family** ? or to an unrecognised one ? seal
s as `degraded` rather than `converged`, and `degraded` does not push. That is the whole of the axis: the round verdict is untouched (it is still NICE, and `g
ate.js` never sees this), and what changes is only *which name the seal gives that NICE*. Families come from `santa/model-diversity.js` classifying the `model
` strings already in the ledger, so it costs no extra reviewer and no extra round. A model string that matches two catalogs at once, or none, is `unknown`, an
d `unknown` degrades ? an unrecognised name must not be able to buy a diversity verdict, and the fix for a legitimately new model is one line added to the cat
alog, not a looser gate.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:968:- What is sealed is `meta.santa_model_families` (distinct family count), `meta.santa_model_degraded`,
 and `meta.santa_degrade_reason` (`same_family` / `unknown_model`) ? all three present-only, and all three written **regardless of the kill switch**, because 
absence of the field means "written before this axis existed" and is a different state from an observed degradation. The reason enum is deliberately narrow: i
t holds only what the ledger projection can derive. Whether Reviewer B fell back because `codex` was *deliberately disabled* or because it was *not installed*
 is a distinction Step 5.5 explains at push time by reading the environment there ? it is guidance for the operator, never a sealed claim, because a fact re-o
bserved at seal time can disagree with what was true when the reviewers ran.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:970:- `MCCP_SANTA_DEGRADE_ACK="<substantive reason>"` lets a degraded run push. It is judged once, inside
 `seal`, against the same strict reason validator the other audited overrides use, and it **does not rewrite the sealed verdict** ? the receipt still says `re
view_verdict='divergent'` with `santa_model_degraded=true`, plus `santa_degrade_ack` and `santa_degrade_ack_reason`. On a machine with no external CLI every r
un degrades and the ack tends to become a resident setting; leaving the verdict alone is what keeps the degraded ratio countable under a resident ack, and tha
t ratio is the measured case for installing `codex`.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:974:- The delta kill switch is `MCCP_SANTA_DELTA_SCOPE` (`off` default / `enforce`), registered in docs/E
NVIRONMENT.md §11 with the other santa toggles. **Its default is the opposite of every other santa toggle, deliberately.** The others default to firing becaus
e a typo that silently reverts a run to pre-axis behaviour is invisible; here firing is the *looser* direction ? it removes files from review ? and the cost o
f being wrong is the 16?93pp detection-rate drop the PRD cites. Measurement **left the default at `off`**, by applying the pre-registered rule rather than by 
judgement. M2 shipped only the deterministic containment layer, so the rule's antecedent ? a *live-reviewer* comparison ? was unmeasured, and an unmeasured an
tecedent is not a satisfied one. That comparison has since been run (2026-08-25): the live lanes found 3 corpus defects at full scope and 2 under the delta, a
nd the rule **refuses a flip on any shortfall** however small. The default is unchanged; what changed is the reason it holds ? `layer2-degraded` (measured, an
d it fell short) rather than `layer2-absent` (never measured). A regression test binds the shipped default to the recorded evidence, so flipping it without th
at evidence turns the suite red.
C:\_project\mccp\plugins\mccp\commands\santa-loop.md:976:- Two present-only integers are sealed: `meta.santa_delta_rounds` and `meta.santa_delta_paths_dropped
`. Both are written **regardless of the kill switch**, so an `off` run stamps `santa_delta_rounds=0` and stays distinguishable from a pre-M1 run whose field i
s simply absent. That distinction is the entire guard against this axis being dark-shipped: with a default of `off`, "nobody ever turned it on" would otherwis
e be silent, and it is now countable across receipts. It does not make the axis fire.

정적 대조를 완료했습니다. 현재 소스는 v1.33.1이며, 제공된 v1.33.0 전제 일부가 이미 드리프트했습니다. 실행 환경에 Node가 없어 테스트 실행은 불가했지만 관련 계약·호출부·지표 producer·receipt corpus를 직접 대조했습니다.
  ✓ 저장소 지침과 관련 파일의 현재 상태 확인
  ✓ 각 finding·recommendation·metric의 증거와 주장 대조
  ✓ 누락 결함 및 confidence 재평가
  → 지정 형식으로 최종 판정 정리
codex
검증 결과는 `divergent`입니다. 핵심 자동화 중단점은 맞지만, 상태 단절·통합 상태 부재·metric 부재 주장은 일부 과장되거나 현재 코드와 어긋납니다.

현재 소스 버전은 제시된 v1.33.0이 아니라 [plugin.json](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/.claude-plugin/plugin.json)의 v1.33.1입니다. Node 실행 파일이 없어 테스트는 실행하지 못했고, 소스·workflow·rece
ipt corpus를 정적으로 대조했습니다.

| 항목 | 판정 | 조정 |
|---|---|---|
| `F1-shell-state-loss` | 부분 사실, 결론 기각 | `REVIEW_DIR` 18회는 정확합니다. 그러나 fresh-shell 상태는 이미 JSON artifact로 전달하도록 계약되어 있습니다([work.md](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mcc
p/commands/work.md:175)). “무인 자동화 불가능”의 직접 증거가 아니므로 confidence `0.95 → 0.45`, severity `HIGH → LOW`가 적절합니다. |
| `F4-plan-phase4-confirmation-halt` | 찬성 | [plan.md](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/commands/plan.md:24), [plan.md](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/commands/pla
n.md:527), [plan.md](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/commands/plan.md:584)이 명시적으로 확인을 기다립니다. 반면 [work.md](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/commands/work.md:139)는 무
확인을 약속합니다. 우회 변수도 없습니다. confidence `0.99` 유지. |
| `F5-command-body-state-fragmentation` | 대부분 기각 | `state-writer`는 이미 state journal을 권위 투영층으로 통합했습니다([state-writer.js](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/scripts/s
tate/state-writer.js:624)). Santa ledger와 evidence ledger는 서로 다른 감사 도메인입니다. 실제 데이터 손실·충돌 사례 없이 “통합 SoT 부재”를 결함으로 단정했습니다. confidence `0.85 → 0.35`. |
| `F6-unattended-metric-absent` | 수정 후 찬성 | A1 work-completion metric 정의는 이미 존재합니다([index.js](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/scripts/lib/msw-metrics/index.js:3
8)). 다만 live startup/completion producer가 없어 `forward-only`입니다([index.js](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mccp/scripts/lib/msw-metrics/index.js:135)). 따라서 “metric이 전
혀 없다”가 아니라 “운영 산출 가능한 `/work` completion metric이 없다”가 정확합니다. confidence `0.80 → 0.97`. |

권고안 판정:

- R1 detached-runner 일반화: 기각합니다. `plan-codex-runner`는 장시간 Codex 실행·nonce·receipt 봉인을 위한 gate 전용 runner입니다. 일반 command fence의 대체물이 아니며 “command당 약 400줄 제거”도 산출
 근거가 없습니다. Effort도 `L`보다 `XL`에 가깝습니다.
- R2 Phase 4 bypass: 방향은 찬성합니다. 다만 상주 환경변수 `yes/no/ask`보다 `/mccp:work` 호출에만 결속된 mode/nonce가 안전합니다. 직접 `/mccp:plan`은 계속 확인하고, work 경로만 확인을 생략하되 Phase 5와 receip
t는 반드시 수행하는 테스트가 필요합니다.
- 제안 metric은 기각합니다. `REVIEW_DIR` 재할당 비율은 구현 세부사항이며 fresh-shell 계약과도 충돌합니다. 대신 `/work started → terminal PR receipt` 완주율, 단계별 halt율, producer coverage를 기록해야 합니
다.

추가로 확인된 사항:

- `work.md`에는 실제 `next-step` 호출이 한 번뿐이고 `record-step` 호출은 0번입니다. 반면 orchestrator는 `record-step` API를 제공합니다([work-orchestrator.js](C:/_project/mccp/plugins/mcc
p/scripts/lib/work-orchestrator.js:318)). 단계별 실패·완주 계측이 기계적으로 연결되지 않은 더 직접적인 결함입니다.
- “72개 ship receipt의 findings가 모두 빈 배열”은 틀립니다. 72개 중 71개가 비어 있고, 1개에는 3개 finding이 있습니다.
- 두 recommendation 모두 `parallel_safe:true`지만 둘 다 `plan.md`를 수정하므로 동시에 안전하지 않습니다.
- CI 수치는 확인됩니다. 테스트 파일 346개 중 workflow가 직접 지정한 테스트 파일은 3개로 약 0.87%입니다.

```text
VERDICT: divergent
DISAGREEMENTS:
- F1-shell-state-loss: 18회 재파생은 사실이나 무인 자동화 차단의 증거는 아님 → JSON artifact handoff가 이미 fresh-shell 계약으로 구현되어 있고 인과·severity가 과장됨 (severity: medium)
- F5-command-body-state-fragmentation: 통합 상태 계약이 없다는 판정에 반대 → state-journal 권위 투영층이 이미 존재하며 도메인별 ledger 분리는 그 자체로 결함이 아님 (severity: medium)
- F6-unattended-metric-absent: metric 자체가 없다는 표현에 반대 → A1 정의는 존재하지만 live producer 부재로 forward-only인 것이 정확함 (severity: low)
- R1-detached-runner-generalization: 전 command fence 일반화와 400줄 절감 효과에 반대 → gate 전용 runner를 상이한 실행 문맥에 과도하게 확장함 (severity: high)
- M1-shell-state-rederivation-frequency: 핵심 성공 metric으로 부적합 → 구현 세부 카운터이며 무인 완주율이나 blocking phase를 측정하지 못함 (severity: medium)
ENDORSED: F4-plan-phase4-confirmation-halt, F6-unattended-metric-absent(corrected), R2-phase4-bypass(corrected)
ADDITIONS:
- work.md는 next-step을 단계마다 호출한다고 선언하지만 기계적 호출은 1회뿐이고 record-step 호출은 0회다; 단계 진행·실패·완주 계측 wiring이 빠져 있다.
- 현재 저장소 버전은 v1.33.1이며, v1.33.0 baseline 전제가 드리프트했다.
- ship receipt findings는 72/72 empty가 아니라 71 empty + 1 receipt/3 findings다.
- 두 recommendation은 plan.md를 함께 수정하므로 둘 다 parallel_safe=true일 수 없다.
- confirmation bypass에는 직접 plan 호출의 확인 유지, work-scoped bypass, Phase 5/receipt 강제 실행을 검증하는 acceptance test가 필요하다.
```
